✨ Ability gained: Fusion Reactor (L1). The System installs your fusion pellet — properly, inside you. You never need to eat again: the pellet covers your metabolism, and your strikes and abilities burn with a steady reactor fire (1.25x, always on). What Victor kept close goes into the ground with them — keepsakes are buried, never taken from the body.
No one is left to pick up the Codex. The village dies out — quietly, the way villages do. The season ends here.
